          String[] retrievedHeader = format.getHeader();
          retrievedHeader[0] = "ModifiedColumn"; // Modify the cloned header
          assertNotEquals("ModifiedColumn", format.getHeader()[0]); // Ensure original is unchanged
      }
    new_imports_code: ''

          String[] retrievedComments = format.getHeaderComments();

      }

    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testPrintRecordWithNullValue() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withNullString("NULL");
          format.printRecord(writer, "value1", null, "value3");

          assertEquals(expectedOutput, writer.toString());

```yaml
test_designs:
  - design_name: |
      Test handling of escape characters in CSV values.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void printAndEscape(CharSequence value, int offset, int len, Appendable out)
      entry_hint: |
        public void printRecord(Appendable out, Object... values)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call printRecord() with a value containing escape characters.
      2. During printing, printAndEscape(...) is reached and the escape handling branch is taken.
      3. The output is generated with the appropriate escape sequences.
    input_focus: |
      Provide a value that includes characters like CR, LF, or the delimiter that require escaping.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the output correctly reflects the escaped characters according to the CSV format rules.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when quote mode is set to NONE.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void printAndEscape(CharSequence value, int offset, int len, Appendable out)
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVFormat withQuoteMode(QuoteMode quoteModePolicy)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call withQuoteMode() with QuoteMode.NONE.
      2. Call printRecord() with a value that contains special characters.
      3. The printAndEscape(...) method is invoked and the branch for no quoting is taken.
    input_focus: |
      Set the quote mode to NONE and provide a value that includes special characters like the delimiter.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the output does not include any quotes around the values and handles special characters correctly.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when quote mode is set to ALL.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void printAndQuote(Object object, CharSequence value, int offset, int len, Appendable out, boolean newRecord)
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVFormat withQuoteMode(QuoteMode quoteModePolicy)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call withQuoteMode() with QuoteMode.ALL.
      2. Call printRecord() with a value that contains special characters.
      3. The printAndQuote(...) method is invoked and the branch for quoting all values is taken.
    input_focus: |
      Set the quote mode to ALL and provide a value that includes special characters.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the output includes quotes around all values, even those that do not require them.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when the delimiter is set to a line break character.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void validate()
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVFormat withDelimiter(char delimiter)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call withDelimiter() with a line break character.
      2. The validate() method is invoked and the branch for invalid delimiter is taken.
    input_focus: |
      Attempt to set the delimiter to a line break character, which should trigger validation.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that an IllegalArgumentException is thrown due to the invalid delimiter.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when the comment marker is set to a line break character.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void validate()
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVFormat withCommentMarker(Character commentMarker)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call withCommentMarker() with a line break character.
      2. The validate() method is invoked and the branch for invalid comment marker is taken.
    input_focus: |
      Attempt to set the comment marker to a line break character, which should trigger validation.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that an IllegalArgumentException is thrown due to the invalid comment marker.

new_tests:
  - test_behavior: Test behavior when formatting an empty record.
    test_name: testFormatEmptyRecord
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testFormatEmptyRecord() {
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT;
          String result = format.format(); // Call with no values
          assertEquals("", result); // Expect an empty string for an empty record
      }
  
  - test_behavior: Test quoting behavior for values containing special characters.
    test_name: testPrintRecordWithSpecialCharacter
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testPrintRecordWithSpecialCharacter() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withQuote('"');
          format.printRecord(writer, "value1,value2", "value3"); // Value with a comma
          String expectedOutput = "\"value1,value2\",\"value3\"" + format.getRecordSeparator();
          assertEquals(expectedOutput, writer.toString());
      }

  - test_behavior: Test handling of a record with a trailing delimiter.
    test_name: testPrintRecordWithTrailingDelimiter
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testPrintRecordWithTrailingDelimiter() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withTrailingDelimiter();
          format.printRecord(writer, "value1", "value2");
          String expectedOutput = "value1,value2" + format.getDelimiter() + format.getRecordSeparator();
          assertEquals(expectedOutput, writer.toString());
      }

  - test_behavior: Test behavior when the delimiter is a special character.
    test_name: testNewFormatWithSpecialCharacterDelimiter
    test_code: |
      @Test(expected = IllegalArgumentException.class)
      public void testNewFormatWithSpecialCharacterDelimiter() {
          CSVFormat.newFormat('|'); // Using pipe as a delimiter
      }

  - test_behavior: Test behavior when a null string is set for null values.
    test_name: testWithNullString
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testWithNullString() {
          String expectedNullString = "N/A";
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withNullString(expectedNullString);

test_designs:
  - design_name: |
      Test handling of null values in header.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private String[] toStringArray(final Object[] values)
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVFormat withHeader(final String... header)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call withHeader() with a header array that includes a null value.
      2. Inside toStringArray(...), the null check for values is reached.
      3. The method processes the header array and handles the null value accordingly.
    input_focus: |
      Provide an array with a mix of valid header strings and a null value.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the resulting header array correctly reflects the input, with null entries handled as expected.

  - design_name: |
      Test whitespace trimming in value parsing.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private CharSequence trim(final CharSequence charSequence)
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVFormat withIgnoreSurroundingSpaces(boolean ignoreSurroundingSpaces)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call withIgnoreSurroundingSpaces(true) to enable trimming.
      2. During value parsing, trim(...) is invoked to handle surrounding spaces.
      3. The trimmed values are processed for output.
    input_focus: |
      Use a CSV input with values that have leading and trailing spaces.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the output values are trimmed of any surrounding whitespace.

  - design_name: |
      Test delimiter conflict with quote character.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void validate()
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVFormat withQuote(final Character quoteChar)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call withQuote() with a character that matches the current delimiter.
      2. Inside validate(), the conflict check for delimiter and quote character is reached.
      3. An IllegalArgumentException is expected to be thrown due to the conflict.
    input_focus: |
      Set the delimiter to a specific character and then attempt to set the quote character to the same value.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the method throws an IllegalArgumentException indicating the conflict.

  - design_name: |
      Test escape character conflict with delimiter.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void validate()
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVFormat withEscape(final Character escape)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call withEscape() with a character that matches the current delimiter.
      2. Inside validate(), the conflict check for delimiter and escape character is reached.
      3. An IllegalArgumentException is expected to be thrown due to the conflict.
    input_focus: |
      Set the delimiter to a specific character and then attempt to set the escape character to the same value.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the method throws an IllegalArgumentException indicating the conflict.

  - design_name: |
      Test comment marker conflict with delimiter.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void validate()
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVFormat withCommentMarker(final Character commentMarker)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call withCommentMarker() with a character that matches the current delimiter.
      2. Inside validate(), the conflict check for delimiter and comment marker is reached.
      3. An IllegalArgumentException is expected to be thrown due to the conflict.
    input_focus: |
      Set the delimiter to a specific character and then attempt to set the comment marker to the same value.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the method throws an IllegalArgumentException indicating the conflict.

```yaml
language: java
existing_test_function_signature: public void printRecord(final Appendable out, final Object... values)
new_tests:
  - test_behavior: Test trimming behavior for values with surrounding spaces.
    test_name: testPrintRecordWithLeadingAndTrailingSpaces
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testPrintRecordWithLeadingAndTrailingSpaces() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT;
          format.printRecord(writer, "  value1  ", "  value2  ", null);
          String expectedOutput = "value1,value2" + format.getRecordSeparator();
          assertEquals(expectedOutput, writer.toString());
      }
    new_imports_code: ''

  - test_behavior: Test quote handling for values requiring encapsulation.
    test_name: testPrintRecordWithValuesNeedingQuotes
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testPrintRecordWithValuesNeedingQuotes() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withQuote('"');
          format.printRecord(writer, "value, with, commas", "value with \"quotes\"", "value with newline\ncharacter");

test_designs:
  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when a non-numeric object is formatted.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void printAndQuote(Object object, CharSequence value, int offset, int len, Appendable out, boolean newRecord)
      entry_hint: |
        public void printRecord(Appendable out, Object... values)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call printRecord() with a mix of numeric and non-numeric objects.
      2. During printing, printAndQuote(...) is reached for the non-numeric object.
      3. The branch handling non-numeric objects is executed.
    input_focus: |
      Provide an array of values that includes a non-numeric object (e.g., a string) alongside numeric values.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the non-numeric object is correctly quoted and formatted in the output.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when formatting an empty value.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void printAndQuote(Object object, CharSequence value, int offset, int len, Appendable out, boolean newRecord)
      entry_hint: |
        public void printRecord(Appendable out, Object... values)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call printRecord() with an empty string as one of the values.
      2. During printing, printAndQuote(...) is reached for the empty string.
      3. The branch handling empty values is executed.
    input_focus: |
      Provide an array of values that includes an empty string.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the output correctly represents the empty value according to the formatting rules.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when a value starts with a comment character.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void printAndQuote(Object object, CharSequence value, int offset, int len, Appendable out, boolean newRecord)
      entry_hint: |
        public void printRecord(Appendable out, Object... values)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call printRecord() with a value that starts with a comment character.
      2. During printing, printAndQuote(...) is reached for that value.
      3. The branch handling values starting with comment characters is executed.
    input_focus: |
      Provide an array of values that includes a string starting with the comment character (e.g., "#").
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the output correctly encapsulates the value to avoid misinterpretation as a comment.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when a value ends with a space.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void printAndQuote(Object object, CharSequence value, int offset, int len, Appendable out, boolean newRecord)
      entry_hint: |
        public void printRecord(Appendable out, Object... values)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call printRecord() with a value that ends with a space.
      2. During printing, printAndQuote(...) is reached for that value.
      3. The branch handling values with trailing spaces is executed.
    input_focus: |
      Provide an array of values that includes a string with trailing spaces.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the output correctly trims or handles the trailing space according to the formatting rules.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when a record separator is specified.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void println(Appendable out)
      entry_hint: |
        public void printRecord(Appendable out, Object... values)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call printRecord() with multiple values to trigger record separation.
      2. During printing, println(...) is reached to handle the record separator.
      3. The branch handling the record separator is executed.
    input_focus: |
      Provide an array of values that will result in multiple records being printed.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the output includes the correct record separator between printed records.

```yaml
language: java
existing_test_function_signature: public void printRecord(Appendable out, Object... values)
new_tests:
  - test_behavior: Test behavior when a non-numeric object is formatted.
    test_name: testPrintRecordWithNonNumericObject
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testPrintRecordWithNonNumericObject() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT;
          format.printRecord(writer, 123, "non-numeric", 456);
          String expectedOutput = "123,\"non-numeric\",456" + format.getRecordSeparator();
          assertEquals(expectedOutput, writer.toString());
      }
    new_imports_code: ''

  - test_behavior: Test behavior when formatting an empty value.
    test_name: testPrintRecordWithEmptyValue
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testPrintRecordWithEmptyValue() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT;
          format.printRecord(writer, "value1", "", "value3");
          String expectedOutput = "value1,,value3" + format.getRecordSeparator();
          assertEquals(expectedOutput, writer.toString());

          String csvContent = "# This is a comment\nvalue1,value2\n# Another comment\nvalue3,value4";
          Reader reader = new StringReader(csvContent);
          CSVParser parser = format.parse(reader);
          assertEquals(2, parser.getRecords().size()); // Only 2 records should be parsed
      }

          CSVParser parser = format.parse(reader);
          assertNull(parser.getRecords().get(0).get(1)); // The NULL string should be treated as null
      }

  - design_name: |
      Test line break handling in CSV formatting.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void print(final Object value, final Appendable out, final boolean newRecord)
      entry_hint: |
        public void printRecord(final Appendable out, final Object... values)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call printRecord() with values that include line break characters.
      2. During printing, print(...) is reached where line break handling is evaluated.
      3. The branch for handling line breaks is executed based on the character checks.
    input_focus: |
      Provide a set of values that includes strings with CR, LF, and other delimiters.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the output correctly escapes or formats the line break characters according to CSV rules.

  - design_name: |
      Test quote handling for numeric and non-numeric values.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void printAndQuote(final Object object, final CharSequence value, final int offset, final int len, final Appendable out, final boolean newRecord)
      entry_hint: |
        public void printRecord(final Appendable out, final Object... values)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call printRecord() with a mix of numeric and non-numeric values.
      2. During printing, printAndQuote(...) is reached where the quote mode is evaluated.
      3. The branch for determining whether to quote based on value type is executed.
    input_focus: |
      Include both numeric values (like integers) and strings that require quoting.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that numeric values are printed without quotes while non-numeric values are correctly encapsulated.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when a comment marker is set.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private boolean isCommentMarkerSet()
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVFormat withCommentMarker(final char commentMarker)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call withCommentMarker() to set a comment marker.
      2. The isCommentMarkerSet() method is invoked to check if the marker is recognized.
      3. The branch for handling the presence of a comment marker is executed.
    input_focus: |
      Set a comment marker character and ensure it is recognized in the format.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the format behaves correctly when a comment marker is present, particularly in parsing scenarios.

  - design_name: |
      Test escaping behavior for special characters in values.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void printAndEscape(final CharSequence value, final int offset, final int len, final Appendable out)
      entry_hint: |
        public void printRecord(final Appendable out, final Object... values)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call printRecord() with values that contain special characters needing escape.

      Provide values that include characters like quotes, delimiters, and line breaks.

        public void printRecord(final Appendable out, final Object... values)

      2. During printing, printAndQuote(...) is reached and the branch for handling quote characters is executed.
    input_focus: |
      Provide a value that includes the quote character, ensuring it is the first value in the record.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the output correctly encapsulates the value with quotes and handles any necessary escaping.

        private void println(final Appendable out)
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVPrinter print(final Appendable out)
